Typing goes to the Jupiter Ace: Shift is its SHIFT, Ctrl or Alt its SYMBOL SHIFT, Backspace deletes, the arrows move the cursor and Esc is BREAK. Click the text to type there again.
Typing goes to the Spectrum. Ctrl is Symbol Shift, Esc is Break. Click the text to type there again. On the keyboard below, click Caps Shift or Symbol Shift and then a key to press them together; both shifts is Extended mode.
Typing goes to the ZX81, whose keys type keywords as its own do: Shift is its SHIFT, Backspace rubs out, the arrows move the cursor, and Esc or Space is BREAK. Click the text to type there again.

With Hyperion's Native Developer Kit for AmigaOS 3.2, resting the pointer on a system function in 68000 or C source shows its prototype, library, offset (_LVO), the registers its arguments go in, and its autodoc; typing _LVO offers the functions. Its headers' structure fields and flags (LN_NAME, MEMF_CHIP) are shown and offered too, and Limoniter's own 68000 assembler finds its headers and _LVO offsets.
